HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0453b6ed9753d49b45cf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:1:1\",\"root_000045\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Alışma, hareket eden topluluğun yine de merkeze bağlı kaldığı bir evcilleştirme benzetmesine kadar genişletilebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_08e47439b1a327562bf7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:1:1\",\"root_000045\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kureyş adı altında çok sayıda parçanın tek bir topluluk halinde toplanması da alışmanın ikincil bir boyutu olarak sezilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0bab1f4bce7fa0712235","text":"3. **Familiarity is a protected expectation.** Fixed `ء ل ف B005` is sharpened by the later `ءَامَنَهُم مِّنْ خَوْفٍ` and `ء م ن B001`, whose scope includes security, trust, and safe-conduct. The opening `لِـ` and the later consequential `فَلْيَعْبُدُوا` place Quraysh's familiarity inside a causal frame, while the final security clause exposes a condition that can make repeated travel feel customary. The assigned role of familiarity is reliable expectation; the role of security is to suppress the feared interruption (`خ و ف B001`). Security -> habituation is my inferred arrow, not an explicit assertion in the packet. This changes the fixed ayah from a statement of sentiment into the naming of a produced, risk-managed regularity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B002","root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2430f6fa6a6d6af6dc08","text":"Later ayat made the opening \"إِيلَاف\" more engineered than I first expected. 106:2 shows that the attachment is routed through seasons and stages; 106:3 shows that it is centered on a house; 106:4 shows that it depends on food and safety. That sequence strengthens ء ل ف B002 beside B005: the root is not only social familiarity but composition after possible separation. The unexpected activator was ع ب د B010 in 106:3: a worship root branch for scattered groups/roads makes the first ayah's ilaf look like an answer to dispersion. 106:4 then sharpens the abductive move: a group can remain \"Quraysh\" in stable attachment only if hunger and fear do not break the circuit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2aaad186065672df7d3b","text":"4. **Habituation as domestication rather than mere custom.** Lexical evidence: ء ل ف B005 includes birds/animals habituated to a place; ر ح ل B004/B005 (dwelling and travelling belongings; fit riding mount); ب ي ت B001/B002 (shelter/household). Structural cue: the ayah's root of familiar attachment is followed by saddled travel and then by \"this house\". Assigned role: animal/place habituation gives an ecological image for repeated departures that still return to a center. Abductive move: I infer that the packet imagines mobility stabilized by attachment, like trained animals or resident birds that range out but remain oriented to home. Reading change: the fixed ayah reads as disciplined attachment: Quraysh are made to belong to a circuit, not merely to enjoy convenience.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B005","root_000551/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4443067b91e6c0aad812","text":"1. **Familiarity is attached to recurrent departure.** The fixed `إِيلَٰف` activates `ء ل ف B005` (familiar attachment, habituation, and staying). Its immediate repetition as `إِۦلَٰفِهِمْ` in `106:2`, now adjacent to `رِحْلَةَ` (`ر ح ل B001`, going forth on a journey) and the winter/summer pair, is the structural cue. The branch's role is to make patterned movement itself familiar, rather than to oppose movement with static residence. The packet supplies repetition, journey, and seasonality; I infer recurrence from their combination. The fixed ayah consequently reads as the cultivation of a dependable mobile habit belonging to Quraysh.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6e4958a083886e78ab32","text":"2. **Composition of scattered routes.** Lexical evidence: ء ل ف B002 (joining different parts), ع ب د B010 in 106:3 (scattering in different directions), ر ح ل B006 (journey stage), and ش ت و B001 / ص ي ف B001 in 106:2 (winter/summer as opposed seasons). Structural cue: the single \"إِيلَاف\" of 106:1 becomes seasonally doubled in 106:2 and morally redirected in 106:3. Assigned role: ء ل ف gathers the seasonal, spatial, and social pieces into one pattern; the scattering branch of ع ب د supplies the alternative condition that worship prevents or organizes. Abductive move: I infer that what needs \"joining\" is not only persons but routes, times, and obligations that could otherwise fly apart. Reading change: the ayah becomes a claim about integrated circulation rather than mere tribal habituation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8941cb2068dc1f82f0c8","text":"2. **The collective is composed by circulation.** The same fixed noun activates `ء ل ف B002` (joining things into a composition and gathering after separation). The opposed seasons in `106:2` separate the circuit temporally, while `هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ` in `106:3` supplies a singular gathering point; the collective name `قُرَيْش` is the social whole produced across those parts. Here the branch acts as a compositional operator joining distinct journey legs, times, and people. The packet never says that every leg returns to the house, so the hub-and-return arrow is abductive, inferred from the seasonal recurrence and the deictically fixed house. `إِيلَٰف` therefore reads as assembled network cohesion, not only interpersonal fondness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8b52f698b595b2ed8b9d","text":"1. **Familiar attachment as a made social condition.** Lexical evidence: ء ل ف B005 (familiar attachment, habituated staying), ء ل ف B002 (joining after separation), and ر ح ل B001/B006 in 106:2 (journeying; stages between stops). Structural cue: 106:1 gives a compact reason phrase, and 106:2 immediately repeats the same root before expanding it into the seasonal journey. Assigned role: B005 explains the settled habit; B002 explains the social composition that lets repeated movement hold together. Abductive move: I infer that \"إِيلَاف\" names not just affection but a produced pattern of reliable attachment across dispersion. Reading change: the fixed ayah reads less like a static privilege and more like the binding of a people into a travel-capable routine.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8f6fabb3e7c5d0ae5d62","text":"The repetition in `106:2` was the first decisive correction: fixed `إِيلَٰف` could no longer be treated as generic affection, because its repeated form is attached directly to a seasonal journey. `106:3` then made `ء ل ف B002` spatial by supplying a gathering center in `هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ`, while `106:4` supplied the more surprising enabling condition, safe-conduct and trust (`ء م ن B001`). The resulting return-to-hub and security -> habituation arrows remain inferred rather than packet-stated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9bd1ca0f5d69456cb767","text":"3. **The thousand/multitude undertone.** Lexical evidence: ء ل ف B001 (thousand as gathered hundreds), ر ب ب B004 in 106:3 (large group or multitude), and ب ي ت B008 (lineage house of honor). Structural cue: \"قُرَيْشٍ\" is the named collective, while later \"رَبَّ هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ\" locates that collective around a house. Assigned role: the numerical/gathering branches let the root gesture toward massing many units under one named body. Abductive move: I infer that the surah's social subject is not merely individuals fond of travel but a collective made countable/gathered by a house-centered order.
